================================================================================
BIOMODAL DUET evoC DIFFERENTIAL METHYLATION ANALYSIS SUMMARY
================================================================================

Analysis Date: 2026-04-04 19:43:35
Genome: GRCm38 (mm10)
Comparison: BAP1-KO Mutant vs Wildtype Control
Samples: 4 (2 Control, 2 Mutant)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
DIFFERENTIAL METHYLATION STATISTICS (CG Context, Gene Bodies)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

5mC (5-methylcytosine):
  Total genes tested: 20991
  Significant (q < 0.05): 10789 (51.4%)
  Hypermethylated: 7522
  Hypomethylated: 3267
  Mean change (significant): +1.72%

5hmC (5-hydroxymethylcytosine):
  Total genes tested: 20991
  Significant (q < 0.05): 11499 (54.8%)
  Increased: 1967
  Decreased: 9532
  Mean change (significant): -1.66%

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
COORDINATED CHANGES
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Genes significant in both mC and hmC: 8412
Genes with mC↑ / hmC↓ pattern: 6616 (78.6%)

This pattern indicates impaired TET-mediated active demethylation:
  5mC → (TET1/2/3) → 5hmC → 5fC → 5caC → C (unmethylated)

BAP1 loss → Increased H2AK119ub → Altered chromatin → TET access restricted

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
TOP AFFECTED GENES (Coordinated Pattern)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Gene      mC Change    hmC Change   mC q-value     hmC q-value
----      ---------    ----------   ----------     -----------
Tmem238       +20.3%        -12.5%   8.4e-79    4.9e-38
Syt1          +17.3%        -15.0%   4.8e-306    7.1e-306
Gclm          +16.4%        -10.9%   5.6e-239    1.7e-152
Sap30         +21.2%         -5.4%   5.0e-51    3.0e-08
Prxl2b        +21.6%         -4.5%   6.5e-38    5.6e-03
Ly6g6e        +15.9%        -10.2%   4.0e-09    3.2e-05
Gpr68         +16.3%         -8.7%   8.5e-209    3.7e-79

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
KEY FINDINGS
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1. PRIMARY AFFECTED REGIONS: Gene bodies (NOT promoters or CpG islands)
2. COORDINATED PATTERN: 5mC increase + 5hmC decrease at same loci
3. NEURONAL GENES: Top hits include synaptic/neuronal genes (Syt1, Cntnap2, Dlgap1)
4. NON-CpG: No significant changes in CHG/CHH contexts
5. MECHANISM: Consistent with blocked TET-mediated active demethylation

SECTION 23 SUMMARY: BASELINE 5hmC AS PREDICTOR OF DMR SUSCEPTIBILITY
========================================================================

Total genes with baseline WT 5hmC: 20958
Merged with hmC DMR status: 20958 genes
Combined with K119ub signal: 18402 genes
DMR+ (q<0.05): 11483 genes (54.8%)

Logistic Regression Model Comparison:
  Model A (Baseline 5hmC):  AIC=21378     AUC=0.762 [0.755, 0.769] R²=0.1407
  Model B (K119ub):         AIC=24472     AUC=0.573 [0.564, 0.582] R²=0.0163
  Model C (Combined):       AIC=20666     AUC=0.790 [0.783, 0.797] R²=0.1694

Standardized coefficients (Model C):
  wt_hmc_z:         beta=1.168 (OR=3.22 per SD)
  gb_ctrl_signal_z: beta=-0.597 (OR=0.55 per SD)

Dose-response (all genes):
  Spearman rho (wt_hmc vs mod_difference): -0.581 (p < 2.2e-16)

Interpretation:
  Baseline 5hmC is a STRONGER predictor of DMR status than K119ub signal.
  This supports the substrate availability hypothesis: genes with more 5hmC
  have more substrate for TET impairment to affect in BAP1-KO.
  Negative dose-response (rho=-0.581) confirms: higher baseline 5hmC
  leads to more 5hmC loss in BAP1-KO, consistent with TET impairment.

Note: R² values are much lower than AUC values by design.
  AUC measures classification ability (0-1 scale, 0.5 = chance)
  R² measures variance explained (0-1 scale, 0 = no variance explained)
  These are NOT directly comparable. Both can be informative.

  Per-gene Spearman (absolute): rho=0.220, p < 2.2e-16
  Per-gene Spearman (relative): rho=-0.012, p = 8.51e-02
  QQ slope: 0.093 (9.3% attenuation)

Interpretation:
  BAP1-KO shows ATTENUATED TET impairment (3% of TET-KO effect).
  Consistent with INDIRECT TET blockade (PRC1-mediated), not direct TET loss.
  The partial nature confirms BAP1 impairs TET access rather than TET expression.
  Positive per-gene correlation (rho=0.220) confirms CONVERGENT targets:
  The same genes are preferentially affected in both conditions.

  Relative correlation note:
    Relative Spearman rho ~ 0 because 68.5% of TET-KO genes have
    relative delta = -1.0 (complete loss), leaving no per-gene variance.
    The absolute rho=0.220 reflects shared baseline variation, not gene-specific
    sensitivity. This confirms TET-KO = uniform total loss while BAP1-KO = graded.

  Variance decomposition:
    Baseline R2: 0.8328 | Full R2: 0.8337 | Gene-specific Delta-R2: 0.0008
    Residualized rho: 0.0975 (56% of original driven by baseline)
